[codex] [codex] ## Sources of Truth
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] - Use [README.md](README.md) for the repository purpose, human entry points, and
[codex] [codex]   documentation index.
[codex] [codex] - Read [docs/local-development.md](docs/local-development.md) before changing
[codex] [codex]   prerequisites, bootstrap behavior, local run commands, TLS setup, or developer
[codex] [codex]   API access.
[codex] [codex] - Use [src/main/resources/application.yml](src/main/resources/application.yml)
[codex] [codex]   for active runtime defaults and property names. Read
[codex] [codex]   [docs/configuration.md](docs/configuration.md) when changing environment
[codex] [codex]   variables, infrastructure integration, caching, schedules, messaging, or
[codex] [codex]   security configuration.
[codex] [codex] - Read [docs/api/README.md](docs/api/README.md) and inspect the controllers before
[codex] [codex]   adding, removing, or reshaping a public endpoint. Treat the runtime OpenAPI
[codex] [codex]   document as the generated API contract.
[codex] [codex] - Read [docs/domain-model.md](docs/domain-model.md) before changing entities,
[codex] [codex]   relationships, domain events, or business rules.
[codex] [codex] - Read [docs/fred-integration.md](docs/fred-integration.md) before changing the
[codex] [codex]   FRED client, provider behavior, import schedule, or rate-limit handling.
[codex] [codex] - Read [docs/advanced-patterns-usage.md](docs/advanced-patterns-usage.md) before
[codex] [codex]   changing providers, distributed locks, caching, domain-event delivery, or
[codex] [codex]   messaging. Consult
[codex] [codex]   [service-common advanced patterns](../service-common/docs/advanced-patterns.md)
[codex] [codex]   when changing the underlying cross-service pattern.
[codex] [codex] - Use [build.gradle.kts](build.gradle.kts),
[codex] [codex]   [gradle/libs.versions.toml](gradle/libs.versions.toml), and the checked-in
[codex] [codex]   Gradle wrapper as the authority for plugins, dependencies, toolchains,
[codex] [codex]   coverage gates, and build behavior.
[codex] [codex] - Read
[codex] [codex]   [service-common Spring Boot conventions](../service-common/docs/spring-boot-conventions.md)
[codex] [codex]   before changing architecture layers, dependency injection, entities, or HTTP
[codex] [codex]   response patterns. Read
[codex] [codex]   [service-common error handling](../service-common/docs/error-handling.md) before
[codex] [codex]   adding exception flows or changing API error responses.
[codex] [codex] - Read
[codex] [codex]   [service-common artifact resolution](../orchestration/docs/development/service-common-artifact-resolution.md)
[codex] [codex]   when local or CI builds cannot resolve shared artifacts. Do not modify or
[codex] [codex]   publish from `../service-common/` from this repository context.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ## Architecture and Security Rules
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] - Keep controllers thin and delegate application behavior to services.
[codex] [codex]   Controllers must never import repositories.
[codex] [codex] - Keep consumers thin and delegate to services. Consumers must never import
[codex] [codex]   repositories.
[codex] [codex] - Keep transaction boundaries in the service layer and persistence behind
[codex] [codex]   repositories.
[codex] [codex] - Use Jakarta Persistence APIs only. Never import `org.hibernate.*` or depend on
[codex] [codex]   Hibernate-specific behavior.
[codex] [codex] - Extend the shared auditable or soft-deletable base entity that matches the
[codex] [codex]   domain lifecycle; consult the shared Spring Boot conventions before creating
[codex] [codex]   an entity.
[codex] [codex] - Access external exchange-rate systems through `ExchangeRateProvider`. The
[codex] [codex]   service layer must never reference FRED-specific clients or types.
[codex] [codex] - Publish domain events from services. Do not inject message publishers into
[codex] [codex]   services; bridge domain events to external messages in listeners.
[codex] [codex] - Apply `@SchedulerLock` to scheduled work that must execute once across pods.
[codex] [codex] - Cache read paths only where the documented cache strategy calls for it, and
[codex] [codex]   evict affected cache entries after imports or mutations.
[codex] [codex] - Preserve claims-header-based authorization and enforce endpoint permissions
[codex] [codex]   with `@PreAuthorize`. Do not trust client-supplied identity or permission
[codex] [codex]   headers outside the platform's validated gateway path.
[codex] [codex] - Treat role definitions and atomic permission ownership as permission-service
[codex] [codex]   concerns. Do not redefine the ecosystem RBAC model in this repository.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ## Java and Test Standards
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] Before writing or modifying Java, read and apply
[codex] [codex] [code-quality-standards.md](../service-common/docs/code-quality-standards.md).
[codex] [codex] Do not skip this step. In particular, use `var` where the standard requires it,
[codex] [codex] use descriptive names, avoid wildcard imports, and end the first sentence of
[codex] [codex] Javadoc with a period.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] Before writing or changing tests, read the relevant section of
[codex] [codex] [testing-patterns.md](../service-common/docs/testing-patterns.md):
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] - Read the unit-testing guidance for isolated service or utility tests.
[codex] [codex] - Read the integration and Testcontainers guidance for persistence, messaging,
[codex] [codex]   cache, or application-context tests.
[codex] [codex] - Test correct behavior and edge cases. Fix defects instead of testing around
[codex] [codex]   them.
[codex] [codex] - Do not weaken, delete, or disable existing tests to make a change pass.
[codex] [codex] - Keep reusable test values in the repository's test fixtures or constants
[codex] [codex]   instead of scattering magic values.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ## Prerequisites and Development Workflow
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] Before implementing a plan or feature:
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] 1. Search the relevant owner documentation for explicit prerequisites.
[codex] [codex] 2. Verify required tools, infrastructure, credentials, and shared artifacts are
[codex] [codex]    available.
[codex] [codex] 3. If a prerequisite is missing, stop and inform the user. Do not invent a
[codex] [codex]    workaround or bypass the dependency.
[codex] [codex] 4. Satisfy the prerequisite in its owning repository or workflow before
[codex] [codex]    returning to the original task. Do not write outside this repository without
[codex] [codex]    separate authorization.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] Use [docs/local-development.md](docs/local-development.md) for the current local
[codex] [codex] setup and run flow. Use the checked-in Gradle wrapper for build tasks.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] When creating an implementation or execution plan for AI Session Handler, read
[codex] [codex] and follow the
[codex] [codex] [AI Session Handler plan format](../ai-session-handler/docs/plan-format.md). Use
[codex] [codex] its canonical template, replace every placeholder, and retain the numbered
[codex] [codex] `## Phase N: Title` headings.
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] Run a specific plan from the repository root with:
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] ```bash
[codex] [codex] ai-session-handler run \
[codex] [codex]   --plan docs/plans/PLAN.md \
[codex] [codex]   --max-phases 999 \
[codex] [codex]   --quiet \
[codex] [codex]   --agent-cmd "../ai-session-handler/.venv/bin/ai-session-handler-codex-high --model MODEL"
[codex] [codex] ```
[codex] [codex] 
[codex] [codex] Omit `--model MODEL` from the quoted agent command to use the wrapper's
[codex] [codex] configured or default model.
[codex] [codex]

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.

[codex] +          cache-provider: basic

[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json
[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..320bbbd1585ee13f76f1f2253ba91b919bc4e568

[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..47e48eae6cb3f2260a94144029c1bf4376a5a0ab
[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}
[codex] diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
[codex] index db59f1018f7b4b5ce5077384512f2bcd723fa8d0..2330a8288027fb66b93fdfc14f213340fca7569d

[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.

[codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] [codex] +pass.

[codex] [codex] @@ -0,0 +1,104 @@
[codex] [codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] [codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] [codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] [codex] +This document records only the `service-common` integration and review checks.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] [codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, and GitHub Actions managers discover this repository's
[codex] [codex] +version catalog, build scripts, wrapper distribution, and workflow actions.
[codex] [codex] +There are currently no Dockerfiles in this repository. Add file-specific
[codex] [codex] +manager configuration only if a future dependency declaration is not covered
[codex] [codex] +by a native manager.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The Phase 3 local extraction found 52 dependency occurrences across 12 files:
[codex] [codex] +33 Gradle occurrences in seven files, one wrapper occurrence, and 18 GitHub
[codex] [codex] +Actions occurrences in four workflows. Maven and Gradle lookups produced 41
[codex] [codex] +candidate branches before scheduling, concurrency limits, and dashboard
[codex] [codex] +approval. Maintained-line and later-major proposals were both visible for the
[codex] [codex] +Spring platform, documentation, test, locking, and wrapper dependencies. The
[codex] [codex] +Actions references were extracted, but authenticated GitHub lookups remain a
[codex] [codex] +post-publication hosted check because no GitHub token is supplied to local
[codex] [codex] +validation.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. It does not turn every
[codex] [codex] +version inherited from the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, or Spring Modulith BOMs
[codex] [codex] +into an independently updateable declaration. The resolved dependency graph
[codex] [codex] +described below is the security inventory for those inherited versions.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] [codex] +In particular, Spring Boot 4 and the related Spring Cloud, Spring Framework,
[codex] [codex] +Spring Security, Spring Modulith, SpringDoc, Testcontainers, ShedLock, and test
[codex] [codex] +platform lines are a compatibility migration, not a routine patch. Renovate
[codex] [codex] +cannot select the correct release-train combination or coordinate consumer
[codex] [codex] +services by itself.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] [codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] [codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] [codex] +`basic` cache provider. Its job token receives only `contents: write`, which is
[codex] [codex] +required by GitHub's Dependency Submission API. The graph is submitted
[codex] [codex] +directly and is not retained as a workflow artifact or published as a Build
[codex] [codex] +Scan.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The action's default resolution task visits all projects and all resolvable
[codex] [codex] +configurations. Do not add project or configuration filters without proving
[codex] [codex] +equivalent coverage: the platform modules contribute imported BOMs, while the
[codex] [codex] +library modules contribute compile, runtime, and test dependency trees.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Local Phase 3 validation generated an official plugin snapshot without
[codex] [codex] +submitting it. The snapshot contained 216 resolved coordinate/version entries
[codex] [codex] +across the four subprojects. It included the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and
[codex] [codex] +Spring Modulith BOM coordinates and these inherited component families where
[codex] [codex] +the current build actually resolves them:
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +| Component family | Resolution evidence |
[codex] [codex] +| --- | --- |
[codex] [codex] +| Spring Framework | Library runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] [codex] +| Spring Security | `service-core` and `service-web` test classpaths; compile-only APIs are also resolved through compile classpaths |
[codex] [codex] +| Embedded Tomcat | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the servlet stack |
[codex] [codex] +| Netty | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the reactive stack |
[codex] [codex] +| Jackson | `service-core` and `service-web` runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +The entry count and resolved versions will change as the checked-in dependency
[codex] [codex] +selection changes; they are evidence from onboarding, not a desired-version
[codex] [codex] +inventory. A successful local snapshot proves generation and transitive
[codex] [codex] +coverage, but only a hosted run after repository activation proves submission
[codex] [codex] +and creates Dependabot alert input.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +GitHub's dependency graph reports resolved packages but does not decide which
[codex] [codex] +BOM or override should remediate an inherited finding. A Dependabot alert is
[codex] [codex] +also not proof that an available Spring Boot update fixes every Framework,
[codex] [codex] +Security, Jackson, Tomcat, or Netty advisory. Trace the affected configuration
[codex] [codex] +with Gradle dependency insight and review any override as a service-owner
[codex] [codex] +decision.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +For every Renovate pull request:
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +1. Keep the shared preset's no-automerge and dashboard-approval behavior.
[codex] [codex] +2. Review the resolved dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring
[codex] [codex] +   release-train compatibility, and whether an inherited advisory is actually
[codex] [codex] +   remediated.
[codex] [codex] +3. Run the repository-required validation in order:
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +   ```bash
[codex] [codex] +   ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] [codex] +   ./gradlew clean build
[codex] [codex] +   ```
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +4. For platform or shared-library changes, validate all consuming services
[codex] [codex] +   before release as required by
[codex] [codex] +   [versioning and compatibility](versioning-and-compatibility.md). Do not
[codex] [codex] +   publish artifacts or update consumers from an unreviewed bot branch.
[codex] [codex] +
[codex] [codex] +Graph generation failures are failures, not clean security results. Preserve
[codex] [codex] +dependency-resolution and submission errors for triage; do not substitute
[codex] [codex] +Maven Local, omit configurations, or enable a hosted Build Scan to make the job
[codex] [codex] +pass.

[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..47e48eae6cb3f2260a94144029c1bf4376a5a0ab
[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json
[codex] new file mode 100644

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.

[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json
[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..320bbbd1585ee13f76f1f2253ba91b919bc4e568

[codex] +  "extends": [
[codex] +    "github>budgetanalyzer/orchestration//renovate-presets/default"
[codex] +  ]
[codex] +}

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6

[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +

[codex] +        shell: bash

[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.

[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json
