[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] +  ]

[codex] import * as core from '@actions/core'

[codex] import * as gradle from '../../execution/gradle'

[codex]     DevelocityConfig,
[codex]     CacheConfig,
[codex]     DependencyGraphConfig,
[codex]     DependencyGraphOption,
[codex]     GradleExecutionConfig,
[codex]     setActionId,
[codex]     WrapperValidationConfig
[codex] } from '../../configuration'
[codex] import {saveDeprecationState} from '../../deprecation-collector'
[codex] import {handleMainActionError} from '../../errors'
[codex] import {forceExit} from '../../force-exit'
[codex] 
[codex] /**
[codex]  * The main entry point for the action, called by Github Actions for the step.
[codex]  */
[codex] export async function run(): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex]     try {
[codex]         setActionId('gradle/actions/dependency-submission')
[codex] 
[codex]         // Configure Gradle environment (Gradle User Home)
[codex]         await setupGradle.setup(new CacheConfig(), new DevelocityConfig(), new WrapperValidationConfig())
[codex] 
[codex]         // Capture the enabled state of dependency-graph
[codex]         const originallyEnabled = process.env['GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED']
[codex] 
[codex]         // Configure the dependency graph submission
[codex]         const config = new DependencyGraphConfig()
[codex]         await dependencyGraph.setup(config)
[codex] 
[codex]         if (config.getDependencyGraphOption() === DependencyGraphOption.DownloadAndSubmit) {
[codex]             // No execution to perform
[codex]             return
[codex]         }
[codex] 
[codex]         // Only execute if arguments have been provided
[codex]         const executionConfig = new GradleExecutionConfig()
[codex]         const taskList = executionConfig.getDependencyResolutionTask()
[codex]         const additionalArgs = executionConfig.getAdditionalArguments()
[codex]         const executionArgs = `
[codex]               -Dorg.gradle.configureondemand=false
[codex]               -Dorg.gradle.dependency.verification=off
[codex]               -Dorg.gradle.unsafe.isolated-projects=false
[codex]               -Dorg.gradle.isolated-projects=false
[codex]               ${taskList}
[codex]               ${additionalArgs}
[codex]         `
[codex]         const args: string[] = parseArgsStringToArgv(executionArgs)
[codex]         await gradle.provisionAndMaybeExecute(
[codex]             executionConfig.getGradleVersion(),
[codex]             executionConfig.getBuildRootDirectory(),
[codex]             args
[codex]         )
[codex] 
[codex]         await dependencyGraph.complete(config)
[codex] 
[codex]         // Reset the enabled state of dependency graph
[codex]         core.exportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED', originallyEnabled)
[codex] 
[codex]         saveDeprecationState()
[codex]     } catch (error) {
[codex]         handleMainActionError(error)
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     // Explicit process.exit() to prevent waiting for hanging promises.
[codex]     await forceExit()
[codex] }

[codex] +      contents: write

[codex] +        with:

[codex] +

[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +

[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json
[codex] new file mode 100644

[codex] +    "github>budgetanalyzer/orchestration//renovate-presets/default"
[codex] +  ]
[codex] +}

[codex] +          cache-provider: basic

[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..77baeb6fdd60c09e8c271d3fe7d8fb4d2b5b42ca

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.

[codex] +      contents: write

[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +

[codex] +

[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Phase 4 local extraction found 57 dependency occurrences across eight files:
[codex] +37 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence, three Dockerfile
[codex] +occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three workflows. Public
[codex] +Gradle, wrapper, and image lookups produced candidate updates. GitHub Actions
[codex] +lookups and the three `org.budgetanalyzer` Maven coordinates remain
[codex] +authenticated hosted checks; the local runner had no GitHub or package-read
[codex] +token, so those checks are pending rather than passed.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '31 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Phase 4 local extraction found 57 dependency occurrences across eight files:
[codex] +37 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence, three Dockerfile
[codex] +occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three workflows. Public
[codex] +Gradle, wrapper, and image lookups produced candidate updates. GitHub Actions
[codex] +lookups and the three `org.budgetanalyzer` Maven coordinates remain
[codex] +authenticated hosted checks; the local runner had no GitHub or package-read
[codex] +token, so those checks are pending rather than passed.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.

[codex] or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Bot pull request checks
[codex] 
[codex] Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] approval.
[codex] 
[codex] Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] ./gradlew clean build
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] `pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] {
[codex]   "$schema": "https://docs.renovatebot.com/renovate-schema.json",
[codex]   "extends": [
[codex]     "github>budgetanalyzer/orchestration//renovate-presets/default"
[codex]   ]
[codex] }

[codex] +      contents: write

[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +

[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-cloud-platform service-core service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `currency-service` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, and
[codex] +`service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up that private Maven coordinate. Before activation, configure
[codex] +the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven credentials and
[codex] +verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] +the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major
[codex] +proposals visible where each datasource supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Phase 4 local extraction found 57 dependency occurrences across eight files:
[codex] +37 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence, three Dockerfile
[codex] +occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three workflows. Public
[codex] +Gradle, wrapper, and image lookups produced candidate updates. GitHub Actions
[codex] +lookups and the three `org.budgetanalyzer` Maven coordinates remain
[codex] +authenticated hosted checks; the local runner had no GitHub or package-read
[codex] +token, so those checks are pending rather than passed.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and Spring Modulith BOMs remain represented by
[codex] +the resolved dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or
[codex] +`serviceCommon` proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is
[codex] +fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] +and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] +alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] +approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] +`pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] +branches to bypass that boundary.

<phase-blocked>Implemented `renovate.json`, the trusted-main weekly dependency-submission workflow, package-access preflight, `docs/dependency-automation.md`, and the README link. The workflow uses basic caching and keeps package credentials separate from `${{ github.token }}`. No dependencies or service logic changed.

Passed: Node 24/npm prerequisite, strict Renovate config validation, actionlint, link checks, and diff checks. Extraction found 57 occurrences across eight files covering Gradle, the wrapper, Dockerfile images, and Actions; all three `org.budgetanalyzer` coordinates were detected. Public lookups returned 47 candidate branches. Authenticated Actions and private-Maven lookups remain pending because this runner has no GitHub/package token.

The official snapshot generator ran, but the snapshot is incomplete: it contains only 87 build/tool coordinates and omits the application runtime/test tree because `serviceCommon` 0.0.16 cannot be remotely resolved without credentials. Gradle confirmed those configurations as failed. Actions run 34027551741 succeeded on the exact 0.0.16 bump commit, establishing that repository secrets exist, but completing this phase requires either exposing the scoped package-read credentials to this runner for a fresh snapshot or committing the prepared workflow and running it successfully on `main`, then confirming GitHub accepted the submission.</phase-blocked>
